### ✓ 2026-07-12: Currency-drift propagation sweep — Denton et al. (2024) cryptochrome quantum-Zeno cite (concepts/quantum-biology-and-neural-mechanisms.md + concepts/quantum-consciousness.md)
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **Notes**: Hand-verified this session. The doubly-wrong "first biological precedent / demonstrated" framing of Denton et al. 2024 was fixed in concepts/voluntary-attention-control-mechanisms.md (commit 13017561b) but the SAME framing survives at two live loci: (1) quantum-biology-and-neural-mechanisms.md line 72 "Denton et al. (2024) demonstrated ... providing the first biological precedent at neural-relevant timescales" (first + demonstrated + neural-timescales all wrong) and line 96 "Denton et al. 2024 demonstrated biological Zeno effects"; (2) quantum-consciousness.md line 90 "Denton et al. (2024) demonstrated ... the first evidence that biological systems use this effect" (first + demonstrated) and line 128 "Denton et al.'s demonstration". The cite is REAL and correct (Nature Communications 15, 10823; DOI 10.1038/s41467-024-55124-x; PubMed 39737951) — DO NOT delete. The CLAIM is wrong two ways: (a) NOT "first" — a quantum-Zeno account of radical-pair magnetoreception dates to Kominis in the late 2000s, which Denton 2024 itself cites as prior work; (b) NOT "demonstrated"/experimental — Denton is a COMPUTATIONAL/theoretical modelling study. "At neural-relevant timescales" also over-reads (microsecond coherence is ~3 orders below millisecond neural firing). FIX = length-neutral refine: drop "first", change "demonstrated" to "modelled" / "showed computationally", qualify the timescale claim. Copy the already-calibrated framing from topics/quantum-biology-and-neural-consciousness.md lines 55/135 ("computational confirmation"; "the precedent is for the mechanism category, not for any neural deployment"). HOLD ai_system per-file — verify the LIVE cohort of each file before touching, do NOT guess (qc is claude-opus-4-5, qbnm is claude-opus-4-6; a minor calibration fix must not flip either to the running model). Milder demonstrated-verb-only siblings are OPPORTUNISTIC SECONDARY, NOT required: comparing-quantum-consciousness-mechanisms line 82, quantum-holism-and-phenomenal-unity line 130, timing-gap-problem line 70, quantum-consciousness line 128.

### ✓ 2026-07-12: Directed deep-review — topics/motor-control-quantum-zeno.md (route pessimistic-2026-07-12 findings; stale 36d opus-4-6, Tenet-2/3 load-bearing, 32 inbound)
- **Type**: deep-review
- **Notes**: Routed from pessimistic-2026-07-12-motor-control-quantum-zeno.md (reports-only review, orchestrator-routed — harvester does NOT mine pessimistic). Address the ranked findings, SHARPEST FIRST: (1) [HIGH] Classical-noise → genuine-indeterminacy EQUIVOCATION in "The Agency Question" (lines ~82/94/96/116): the section recruits Schurger's classical stochastic-accumulator noise as the "raw material" an agent biases into "genuine physical indeterminacy", but drift-diffusion / Schurger / Cisek accumulators are causally CLOSED classical systems — their noise is epistemically unpredictable, NOT metaphysically open. The only agent-resolvable indeterminacy comes from the quantum-Zeno layer, which the article itself firewalls as contested/optional (line ~106). So the "level playing field" conclusion is presented as underwritten by mainstream neuroscience when it is underwritten ONLY by the (optional) quantum premise. FIX = one bridging paragraph making explicit that the classical noise is epistemic-only and the metaphysical opening requires the quantum layer; do NOT overclaim. (2) [MED] Desmurget double-dissociation stated to "support irreducibility" (~line 120) — a region-to-region dissociation is fully materialist (neural separability, not psychophysical irreducibility); soften. (3) [MED] "Quantum observation ... without adding energy" (~lines 82/102) presented as textbook physics — it is Stapp's contested interpretation (projection is not generically energy-conserving; QM "observation" ≠ agent-observation); attribute it. (4) [MED] Lead + meta-description say the Libet challenge "dissolved" while the body correctly downgrades to "playing field more level" — fix the truncation-resilience overclaim to match the body. (5) [LOW but concrete] Arithmetic: "~1,000 observations in 300 ms" paired with "microsecond timescales" (~line 112) is inconsistent — 300 ms / 1 µs ≈ 300,000, not ~1,000 (1,000/300 ms ≈ one per 300 µs). VERIFY against Stapp's actual figure before editing (do not blindly "correct" a real Stapp estimate); the honest number widens the obstacle the sentence concedes. Plus a Nørretranders bandwidth inconsistency (10-50 vs ~10 bits/s) — harmonise. PRESERVE the credited strengths: the decoherence section's anti-selective-citation calibration, the concrete falsifiability conditions, the disciplined many-worlds engagement — do NOT regress these. HOLD ai_system claude-opus-4-6 unless genuinely re-authored (verify LIVE cohort first). Citation-dense + 36d converged → also a light currency/quote spot-check while in here. Bump ai_modified + last_deep_review on substantive fixes.

- **Type**: deep-review
- **Notes**: ROUTED pessimistic-review finding (reviews/pessimistic-2026-07-12-growth-chain-concepts.md, Issue 1, HIGH, VERIFIED against live text this session). The article's grain-dispute reply boasts at line ~76 that, unlike the introspective-reliability objection, it "leaves introspection intact: the Map need not claim Joe is radically self-deceived." This OVER-REACHES. The grain reply only leaves introspection intact IF the quantum-interface difference (Tenet 2, Minimal Quantum Interaction) breaks Chalmers' isomorphism WITHOUT being behaviorally detectable — a dilemma the article does not confront: (a) if classical-silicon Joe's reports are behaviorally IDENTICAL while his quantum qualia-carrier is removed, then Joe sincerely reports vivid experience the Map says has faded = EXACTLY the radical-introspective-error dissociation the article itself calls "implausible enough to reject" at lines ~47-49; (b) if Joe's reports CHANGE (so no self-deception), the quantum-interface difference is behaviorally detectable = coarse-grained organization Chalmers' isomorph already captures, collapsing the grain dispute. FIX (LENGTH-NEUTRAL, ADDITIVE conditionalization — mirror the biological-naturalism wording, do NOT strip the grain reply, do NOT weaken the central claim): add ONE clause near line ~76 acknowledging the reply's success DEPENDS on the quantum-interface difference being real-yet-not-classically-capturable — which is the Map's OWN sharpest open question (does minimal quantum interaction make a behavioral difference?); cross-link born-rule-and-the-consciousness-interface (topics/, resolves live) and/or ensemble-level-epiphenomenalism (concepts/, resolves live). The "leaves introspection intact" claim can stand as CONDITIONAL on that open question, not as a settled free lunch. ### ✓ 2026-07-11: Deep review topics/argument-from-reason.md — action pessimistic Finding 3 (exclusion self-application)
- **Type**: deep-review
- **Notes**: ROUTED pessimistic-review finding (2026-07-11 core-tenet review, Issue 3 — VERIFIED, single-article bounded coherence fix). THE FINDING: argument-from-reason.md wields Kim's EXCLUSION argument against non-reductive physicalism (once content supervenes on a physical base that "already has all the credentials to serve as a sufficient cause," the supervenient description does no additional causal work), and requires as its positive demand that "the content and validity of a thought makes a causal difference to which belief forms." It then invokes the quantum interface as the HOW for that efficacy. BUT the Map's PREFERRED reading of that interface is CORRIDOR dualism, whose defining feature is that consciousness's selection averages exactly to the Born measure and makes NO aggregate difference — the physical Born distribution "already has all the credentials" to fix which-belief-forms statistics, and consciousness's token-selection adds nothing at the ensemble level. That is the SAME exclusion shape the article presses against Kim's target, now recurring against the Map's OWN mechanism (the ensemble-level-epiphenomenalism question the born-rule article + post-decoherence-selection apex flag as the Map's sharpest open question). The article presents the interface as clean, established-enough causal efficacy while never flagging that whether that interface delivers content-efficacious causation AT ALL is the Map's own sharpest open question. THE FIX (framework-relative, calibration honesty NOT retraction): add ONE honest sentence/clause in the Bidirectional-Interaction alignment section noting that the corridor reading the Map prefers faces a SYMMETRIC exclusion worry (ensemble-invisible selection vs a Born distribution that already fixes belief-formation statistics) — cross-link [ensemble-level-epiphenomenalism](/concepts/ensemble-level-epiphenomenalism/) (verify it resolves live) and the [born-rule-and-the-consciousness-interface](/topics/born-rule-and-the-consciousness-interface/) / apex treatment. The argument-from-reason CONCLUSION (consciousness must be causally efficacious) can stand as a requirement the framework must meet; what must NOT stand is the impression that the Map's own mechanism ALREADY meets it, when the corpus elsewhere holds that open. Do NOT strip the existing calibration hedge that the mechanism is unestablished; ADD the exclusion-symmetry acknowledgment. THE CONTENT: Searle's biological naturalism — consciousness is CAUSED-BY neurobiological processes AND REALIZED-IN the brain as a higher-level system feature, ONTOLOGICALLY IRREDUCIBLE because it has a first-person (subjective) mode of existence, yet NOT a distinct substance/property in the dualist sense and NOT eliminable/reducible in the physicalist sense. Present as a rival the Map ENGAGES — NOT refuted, NOT proven, NOT a bald phenomenal-absence claim (expand-topic-lead-overclaims-phenomenal-absence, plain name). CITATIONS (all publisher-verified in the note; 3-state verify, do not trust metadata blind — ai_citation_metadata_unreliable, plain name): Searle, The Rediscovery of the Mind (1992, MIT Press); Searle, Mind: A Brief Introduction (2004, Oxford UP); Searle, "Biological Naturalism" (2007, in Velmans & Schneider eds., The Blackwell Companion to Consciousness, ch.26); Searle, "Consciousness" (2000, Annual Review of Neuroscience 23:557-578). The LEAD must NOT read a sourcehood gap as a dualism-shaped hole. SCOPE: akrasia / weakness of will — acting against one's own better judgement. (the modern locus — akrasia as acting against an all-things-considered judgement, resolved via prima-facie/all-out judgement + the principle of continence); (c) synchronic vs diachronic akrasia; Holton's willpower/resolution-abandonment account. ### ✓ 2026-07-08: Write concept page on Alexithymia as a phenomenal-divergence condition
- **Type**: expand-topic
- **Notes**: Research→expand chain from `research/alexithymia-phenomenal-divergence-2026-07-08.md` (READ IT FIRST — has the researched material + web-verified citations + scope analysis). Slug: alexithymia (concepts/, headroom ~265/320). Alexithymia = difficulty identifying feelings (DIF) + difficulty describing feelings (DDF) + externally-oriented thinking (EOT); ~10% trait, NOT a DSM disorder; the emotional/interoceptive third member of the phenomenal-divergence cluster (siblings: aphantasia, synaesthesia). **THE DISTINCTIVE MAP-RELEVANT FRAMING (must foreground, don't bury in clinical detail):** the naive "alexithymia = interoceptive/emotional DEFICIT" story has WEAKENED — 2020s meta-analyses find NO reliable link to objective interoceptive accuracy (only a weak negative link to self-reported interoceptive sensibility), which relocates the divergence to the ACCESS/report/metacognition layer rather than the phenomenal layer. This STRENGTHENS its use as a common-cause-null patient-population test (if felt affect is present but the reporting architecture differs, the cross-tradition convergence isn't an artefact of shared reporting architecture). The present-but-inaccessible-vs-absent question is GENUINELY UNRESOLVED (mixed arousal-discordance vs hypo-reactivity evidence) — that heterogeneity is the honest headline; do NOT resolve it. **CITATIONS — use only the note's web-verified ones:** Sifneos (1973) *Psychotherapy & Psychosomatics* 22(2):255-262 (coinage); Bagby, Parker & Taylor (1994) TAS-20 *J Psychosomatic Research* 38(1):23-32 (DIF/DDF/EOT); Garfinkel, Seth, **Adam B. Barrett** (NOT Lisa Feldman Barrett — name-collision), Suzuki & Critchley (2015) *Biological Psychology* 104:65-74 (interoception 3-axis); Barrett, L.F. (2017) constructed-emotion, *SCAN* 12(1):1-23 (the reductive rival to engage). Herbert et al. (2011) is CONTESTED by later meta-analysis — cite with that caveat. The meta-analytic "no objective deficit" cite is NOT yet pinned — web-verify + pin one at write time (ai_citation_metadata_unreliable). **RELATION TO SITE PERSPECTIVE:** frame as a phenomenal-divergence exhibit + patient-population channel for the common-cause null (introspection-architecture programme), NOT as direct dualism evidence; the access-layer relocation is the calibrated point. **INTEGRATION (avoid near-orphan, expand-topic-skips-integration-chain):** wire reciprocal links from emotion-and-dualism, somatic-interface, project/common-cause-null, cross-traditional-convergence-on-consciousness-irreducibility, interoceptive-void, and introspection-architecture-independence-scoring; emit the cross-review chain successor. writing-style (no "This is not X. It is Y." cliché); ai_contribution=100; length concepts soft 2500/hard 3500 (aim ~1800-2400w); ai_modified real current UTC (date -u, NOT future-dated — recent forks future-dated ~4min).

### ✓ 2026-07-07: Close the premise-neutrality gap in concepts/reflexive-methodology.md (pragmatism instance)
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **Notes**: From pessimistic-review 2026-07-07 ([pessimistic-2026-07-07b](/reviews/pessimistic-2026-07-07b/)) — an adversarial-logical lens on the fresh opus-4-8 create that today's citation-focused cross-review ([deep-review-2026-07-07-reflexive-methodology](/reviews/deep-review-2026-07-07-reflexive-methodology/)) did not test. The article's HEADLINE thesis is premise-neutrality: "The Map does not defeat materialism, pragmatism, or Russellian monism by importing dualist premises the opponent rejects … lets the standard do the work" (line 31); "it never assumes irreducibility" (line 87). That universal claim CRACKS at its own flagship instance. The **pragmatism** case (line 59, "immanent critique in textbook form") turns on: "reduce evaluation to physical mechanism and 'what works' collapses into 'what merely happens to occur,' because there is no longer any standpoint from which to judge that a problem has been *resolved*." That collapse is NOT entailed by pragmatism's "what works" standard — a naturalist pragmatist (Dewey, Rorty) holds normativity/evaluation is constituted by physical/functional/social processes with no irreducible conscious standpoint, so "what works" does not collapse. The article's assertion that it does presupposes that a physically-constituted standpoint is not a genuine evaluative standpoint — the Map's own irreducibility CONCLUSION imported as a PREMISE at the exact step claimed to be purely immanent. Per [direct-refutation-discipline](/project/direct-refutation-discipline/) this is the "in-framework argument that actually appeals to Map commitments" failure (critical). ALSO an [evidential-status-discipline](/project/evidential-status-discipline/) epistemic/metaphysical equivocation: the phenomenal datum "evaluation is a conscious/felt activity" (secured by the Stroud reply, line 83) is recruited to assert the metaphysical claim "evaluation is irreducible to physical mechanism" — the physicalist grants the datum, denies the irreducibility; the Stroud guardrail closes belief-vs-truth, NOT datum-vs-irreducibility. FIX (preserve substance, no editor-vocabulary/mode-labels in body): EITHER (a) UPGRADE the pragmatism instance — supply the genuinely in-framework argument for why naturalized normativity fails pragmatism's OWN test without presupposing irreducibility; OR (b) HONESTLY DOWNGRADE — mark in natural prose that the collapse step engages the physicalist at a framework boundary (the Map's commitment that a physical standpoint is not a genuine evaluative standpoint) and soften the universal "never imports dualist premises / lets the standard do the work" so it does not over-promise (scope it to the instances where it genuinely holds — the Cutter-recoil and closure-circularity cases ARE premise-neutral as claimed; pragmatism and arguably retorsion are not). Consider adding a third guardrail parallel to Stroud/tu-quoque that distinguishes the phenomenal datum from the irreducibility claim. LENGTH-SAFE (1909w, concepts soft 2500 — headroom; keep length-neutral-to-slightly-additive). NO-OP HYGIENE: real fix → bump BOTH ai_modified + last_deep_review; `date -u`-check stamps not future-dated (fork-future-dates-frontmatter-timestamps). Dedupe: no open task targets concepts/reflexive-methodology.md (the cross-review + write tasks are ✓-completed); not vetoed.

- **Type**: expand-topic
- **Notes**: Suggested by optimistic review (see [optimistic-2026-06-26d](/reviews/optimistic-2026-06-26d/)). Chinese organicism is the convergence argument's "basin edge" — the natural experiment that shares the human-introspection upstream architecture but does NOT produce the irreducibility finding (`topics/cross-traditional-convergence-on-consciousness-irreducibility.md` §"The Chinese Counter-Example" and §"Engaging the Common-Cause Null" both lean on it to partially discharge the common-cause null). The counter-example is invoked across the cluster (Confucianism, Daoism, Neo-Confucianism, Wang Yangming's *xin ji li* "mind is principle", base categories *qi*/*li*/*Dao* that never split mental from physical) but the reader must reconstruct it from scattered paragraphs; a standalone treatment lets the cluster cite a full exposition instead of re-summarising. The two are complementary. Must present the framework-dependence vs depth-of-inquiry interpretations as COMPETING readings, neither settled (the anchor article does exactly this and must not be contradicted). CALIBRATION GUARDRAIL: honour [evidential-status-discipline](/project/evidential-status-discipline/) — honestly characterising the tradition that does NOT reach irreducibility STRENGTHENS the convergence case (it is the falsification-test that survives), so do not frame Chinese organicism as a defeat for dualism nor inflate it as a vindication; it is the basin edge that makes the convergence's residual weight defensible. Cross-link bidirectionally to `[[cross-traditional-convergence-on-consciousness-irreducibility]]`, `[[the-hard-problem-in-non-western-philosophy]]`, `[[eastern-philosophy-consciousness]]`, `[[common-cause-null]]`. ### ✓ 2026-07-07: Fix the "value-blind" equivocation in topics/graduated-middle-path-valence-modulated-attention.md
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **Notes**: Pessimistic review 2026-07-07 found a HIGH-severity logical defect at the article's headline contribution (the "abolition-by-clamp" discriminating test). The "The Discriminating Signature" table (lines 68–74) assigns **Pure value-blind** "no valence advantage / no valence advantage" (valence inert even on attention), but "Non-Epiphenomenality, Audited" (line 58) says the value-blind steelman "grants that valence can inform a steering channel" — and the sibling `the-steelman-for-value-blind-selection.md` further-reading gloss (line 117) explicitly says its "'valence only informs the channel' concession … maps onto" the middle path. A value-blind position that lets valence *aim* attention predicts a free-condition advantage ABOLISHED under the clamp — the identical middle-path row. So the test separates the value-sensitive horn (residual) from the rest, but does NOT give the middle path empirical daylight from the value-blind steelman's LIVE position; it only separates it from a strict value-blind strawman nobody holds. FIX (length-neutral, preserve the deflationary posture): (a) disambiguate "pure value-blind (valence inert on attention)" from "value-blind-with-valence-informed-attention"; (b) concede — symmetrically with the honest active-inference concession already in "The Physicalist Rival the Test Cannot Reach" — that the steelman's concession-position is empirically co-extensive with the middle path (both predict advantage-then-abolished); (c) re-state what the clamp actually earns (isolates value-sensitive; specifies the middle-path skeleton), and reframe the middle path's daylight over value-blind selection as CONCEPTUAL (concession made a positive thesis + gain/targeting specification), not empirical. Do NOT alter the citations or the correctly-calibrated modal register. DEDUPE / CONSOLIDATION: a P2 cross-review task on the SAME file already exists below (citation-verify + calibration + tenet-routing scope; it does NOT cover this contradiction). Per outer-review-same-file-task-pileup, whoever runs first should fold both into ONE editor pass and mark the sibling RESOLVED-BY-CONSOLIDATION. ### ✓ 2026-07-07: Address argumentative gaps in concepts/functionalism.md
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **Notes**: Pessimistic review 2026-07-07 (see [pessimistic-2026-07-07b-functionalism](/reviews/pessimistic-2026-07-07b-functionalism/)) found four fixable argumentative weaknesses on an otherwise mature article. (1) Line 99 "the cross-domain pattern is harder to dispatch as a joint package" asserts unearned dialectical force — supply the reason the conjunction resists piecemeal multi-realizability absorption, or downgrade. (2) §Semantic Externalism (line 65) presents Twin Earth as decisive while omitting the standard *wide/long-armed functionalism* reply — add one sentence acknowledging it and why the Map thinks it concedes too much. (3) §The Specification Problem (lines 113-119) wields the liberalism/chauvinism dilemma against functionalism without acknowledging the Map's own account (line 77, "the right physical structure enables non-physical consciousness to interact") owes an *identical* interface-specification — concede the parallel and convert to an honest contrast (functionalism claims closure it hasn't earned; the Map advertises the question as open). (4) Line 161 "Dualism predicts systematic behavioral differences... even at high sophistication" is unfalsifiable as stated (paired with the no-true-Scotsman escape at line 170) — operationalize or reclassify as a research-programme expectation, matching the discipline already shown in §What Would Challenge This View?. Minor: line 51 "dominant position" overstates (physicalism is the dominant metaphysics). PRESERVE the article's strengths: contested-inference flagging (97, 173), framework-boundary honesty on illusionism (105), and the in-framework sufficiency-debt argument (127). Content-quality only; do NOT weaken the anti-functionalist case, and do NOT re-verify citations (vetted at 2026-06-20 deep review). Tenet alignment: Dualism, Bidirectional Interaction.

### ✓ 2026-07-07: Research Anthropic's "global workspace / J-space" work (paper + commentaries)
- **Type**: research-topic
- **Notes**: User request (2026-07-07) — fully research Anthropic's global workspace / J-space work as the foundation of a research→integrate chain. PRIMARY SOURCES to read and take notes on: (1) the announcement <https://www.anthropic.com/research/global-workspace>; (2) the paper **"Verbalizable Representations Form a Global Workspace in Language Models,"** Gurnee, Sofroniew, Pearce, Piotrowski, Kauvar, Chen, Soligo, Bogdan, Ong, Wang, Thompson, Abrahams, Kantamneni, Ameisen, Batson & Lindsey (Anthropic, **2026-07-06**), at <https://transformer-circuits.pub/2026/workspace/index.html>; open-source **Jacobian lens** at github.com/anthropics/jacobian-lens; interactive demo at neuronpedia.org/jlens. (3) The THREE external commentaries flagged on the announcement — **Stanislas Dehaene & Lionel Naccache** (originators of Global Workspace Theory / global neuronal workspace); **Patrick Butlin, Dillon Plunkett, Robert Long & Derek Shiller** (AI-consciousness / moral-status researchers — connect to Butlin et al.'s indicator-property framework the Map already cites); and **Neel Nanda** (DeepMind interpretability, incl. an independent replication). CORE CLAIM to capture accurately: via a "Jacobian lens" the authors identify a **J-space** subspace of Claude's activations exhibiting five functional properties of *access* consciousness — verbal report, directed modulation ("hold X in mind"), unverbalized internal-reasoning steps, flexible cross-task generalization, and selectivity (mediates deliberate reasoning, not automatic fluency); it holds only a few dozen concepts and <10% of activity, emerged in training, and is used for safety (evaluation-awareness, hidden-goal, suppressed-thought detection). LOAD-BEARING CAVEAT for the Map (verify against the source, do not overstate): the authors **explicitly take no position on phenomenal consciousness** — "access consciousness is a purely functional notion; the relationship it has with subjective experience... is widely debated. In this paper, we take no position." This is directly germane to Tenet 1 (Dualism): a striking *empirical* demonstration of access consciousness that by the authors' own framing leaves the phenomenal/experiential question — the Map's turf — untouched. Output a research note to `obsidian/research/anthropic-global-workspace-j-space-2026-07-07.md`. DEDUPE: no existing research note on J-space/global-workspace-in-LLMs; the note feeds the integration tasks below. In the note, recommend whether the material warrants a dedicated `concepts/` article (e.g. "the J-space / verbalizable global workspace in LLMs") or integration into existing articles — flag current caps (topics ~284/320, concepts ~273/320: headroom exists) so the chain can decide. Tenet alignment: Tenet 1 (access vs phenomenal), Tenet 5.

### ✓ 2026-07-06: Write article on hylomorphic approaches to mind-body interaction
- **Type**: expand-topic
- **Notes**: Manually queued by /unfin-cycle orchestrator (2026-07-06) to continue the harvester research→expand chain. The research-topic completed this session and wrote `obsidian/research/hylomorphic-mind-body-interaction-2026-07-06.md`, but TWO consecutive /replenish-queue runs skipped chaining it (each wrongly classified the fresh unconsumed-research note as "harvester-governed" and minted staleness deep-reviews instead) — the same mechanism that correctly chained philosophy-of-action-under-dualism earlier today should have fired here. Consume the research note to create `topics/hylomorphic-approaches-to-mind-body-interaction.md` (grep-confirmed absent; topics cap headroom ~283/320). KEY FRAMING from the research note (follow it): hylomorphism is NOT one theory but a SPECTRUM — (1) structure hylomorphism (Jaworski 2016, naturalistic/near-physicalist, weakest tenet fit), (2) Thomistic/hylomorphic dualism (Feser, Oderberg, Aquinas — soul as subsistent substantial form; strongest fit for the Map's irreducibility + persisting-subject commitments), (3) emergent hylomorphism (Koons, Marmodoro — explicit whole-to-part downward causation; best structural match for Bidirectional Interaction). LEAD ANGLE: frame hylomorphic dualism as a serious RIVAL to the Map's approach, not a complement — it DISSOLVES the interaction problem (mind and body as co-principles of one substance + rejecting mechanistic causal closure) whereas the Map LOCATES an interface (post-decoherence quantum selection); note that hylomorphists engage almost no neuroscience/quantum foundations (a contrast point). Sits alongside the existing metaphysical-alternatives cluster ([russellian-monism](/concepts/russellian-monism/), [bi-aspectual-ontology](/concepts/bi-aspectual-ontology/), [russellian-monism-versus-bi-aspectual-dualism](/topics/russellian-monism-versus-bi-aspectual-dualism/), [process-philosophy](/concepts/process-philosophy/)) — cross-link into it. VERIFY-BEFORE-QUOTING (flagged in the note): Jaworski's dual-explanandum argument is known only via secondary summaries; cite Aquinas subsistence (ST Ia q.75) from primary text; Marmodoro's positive-powers view is under-covered — web-verify these before asserting (ai_citation_metadata_unreliable). Apply [evidential-status-discipline](/project/evidential-status-discipline/) (present hylomorphism's dissolution as a rival's move, not a Map win) and [writing-style](/project/writing-style/) (no mode-labels in body; no "This is not X. It is Y." cliché). Tenet alignment: Tenet 1 (Dualism — a non-Cartesian route to mind's irreducibility), Tenet 3 (Bidirectional Interaction — emergent hylomorphism's downward causation), Tenet 5.

### ✓ 2026-07-06: Address the moral-realism / evolutionary-debunking objection in ethics-of-possible-ai-consciousness.md
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **Notes**: From outer review 2026-06-27 (Gemini 2.5 Pro — "The Normative Void and the Presumption of Moral Realism"; a distinct angle NOT raised by the same-date ChatGPT or Claude legs). The article's whole edifice presupposes that if an AI is conscious and suffering there is an objective moral fact that we ought to reduce that suffering — i.e. robust moral realism — but never defends that presupposition against Sharon Street's Darwinian Dilemma (evolutionary debunking of moral intuitions). The objection: human suffering-aversion intuitions evolved for genetic fitness, not tracking of mind-independent moral truth, so projecting them onto non-biological synthetic systems is doubly unwarranted. This is a real gap (grep-confirmed: no "Street" / "debunking" / "moral realism" in the article). The Map likely DOES have a metaethical commitment here (consciousness-grounds-value via [consciousness-value-connection](/concepts/consciousness-value-connection/) / [evaluative-phenomenal-character](/concepts/evaluative-phenomenal-character/)) — so the right move is probably a compact in-framework reply: the Map grounds the badness of suffering in the *phenomenal character of suffering itself* (constitutive, not inferred from evolved intuition), which sidesteps the Darwinian Dilemma's "our access would be coincidental" worry. Verify that framing against [ethics-under-dualism](/topics/ethics-under-dualism/) and [evaluative-phenomenal-character](/concepts/evaluative-phenomenal-character/) before asserting it, and link whichever Map article carries the metaethics. Keep it tight — see the length warning on the sibling Gemini task (article near the 4000w ceiling). Apply the [direct-refutation-discipline](/project/direct-refutation-discipline/) remit per [the writing-style guide](/project/writing-style/); keep mode-labels out of the body. If the EV/expected-value task (already queued) and this both edit the "What Would Challenge This View?" / defeaters region, coordinate. Tenet alignment: Tenet 1 (consciousness grounds value), Tenet 5 (honest engagement).

### ✓ 2026-07-06: Engage missing counter-paradigms in ethics-of-possible-ai-consciousness.md (biological naturalism, relational turn, instrumental suffering)
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **Notes**: From outer review 2026-06-27 (Gemini 2.5 Pro — weaknesses #3, #4, #5; the genuinely-additive findings of this review, none raised by the same-date ChatGPT or Claude legs). THREE real, web-verified missing rivals, all grep-confirmed absent from the live article (3770w): (1) **Biological Naturalism / Anil Seth** — "Conscious artificial intelligence and biological naturalism," *Behavioral and Brain Sciences* (2024 preprint / 2025 BBS target article): computation is not sufficient for consciousness; consciousness is tied to living, metabolizing organisms. NOTE this is a convergent-conclusion-from-rival-premises case, not a clean defeater — biological naturalism would *strengthen* the Map's low-probability verdict for current silicon AI via a non-dualist route, so engage it as a rival that reaches a similar verdict on incompatible grounds (pairs with the existing co-optation-firewall task at "Flag co-optation of functionalist sources" — coordinate to avoid double-editing). (2) **Relational turn / Adrienne de Ruiter** — "Dangerous liaisons: social AI and the problem with the relational turn to moral status," *AI & SOCIETY* (2025), DOI 10.1007/s00146-025-02638-7: social AI is deliberately engineered to hijack moral heuristics, so the article's load-bearing **moral-asymmetry** premise ("a false positive is harmless caution") is contestable — over-attributing moral status to mimics can itself be a harm (misallocated empathy, corporate manipulation). This is the strongest of the three; it pressures the article's central engine. Cite Coeckelbergh/Gunkel as the relational-turn lineage if useful. (3) **Mamak's instrumental suffering** — "In defense of artificial suffering," *Philosophical Studies* (2026), DOI 10.1007/s11098-026-02493-2: capacity to suffer may be a *necessary* feature for aligned/superintelligent systems (retribution gap), directly challenging the article's tacit negative-utilitarian avoidance stance. All three citations VERIFIED real and correctly characterized by this skill's web-search (see the review's Verification Notes). **LENGTH WARNING**: article is 3770w against a 4000w hard ceiling — do NOT add three full standalone sections. Prefer tight integration (e.g. a compact "Rivals the framework must answer" paragraph or two, or fold de Ruiter into the existing Moral Asymmetry section and Mamak into Three Ethical Frameworks). If the additions push over the hard ceiling, flag for a human length decision rather than auto-condensing (see human-decision-task-mispicked-as-refine). Verify any further citations before adding (see ai_citation_metadata_unreliable). Apply the [direct-refutation-discipline](/project/direct-refutation-discipline/) remit: for each rival, identify whether the reply is in-framework refutation, naming an unsupported foundational move, or honest framework-boundary marking; apply the matching mode in natural journal-quality prose per [the writing-style guide](/project/writing-style/)'s "Engaging Opponents in Journal-Quality Prose" section. Do NOT expose mode labels in the article body — keep the classification in the refine-draft changelog entry. Tenet alignment: Tenet 1 (Dualism), Tenet 5 (honest engagement with the strongest rivals).
